Product altitude, no code scan. Use when a PRD exists; for a new one, use /create-prd. ([ihudak/ai-workflows](https://github.com/ihudak/ai-workflows/blob/HEAD/plugins/product-workflows/commands/update-prd.md) · ★2)
- **update-prd**: PRD-update workflow (PM phase) — refresh/re-do an existing Product Requirements Document. - **uv-lock**: Generate or update the uv lockfile by running `uv lock`. ([marcopellegrinoit/gcp-mlops-customer-churn](https://github.com/marcopellegrinoit/gcp-mlops-customer-churn/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/uv-lock.md))
- **uv-pip**: `uv pip` 提供与 pip 完全兼容的命令行接口，但速度更快（10-100倍）。可以直接替换 pip 命令，无需修改工作流程。 ([trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills](https://github.com/trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/uv-python-manager/references/commands/uv-pip.md) · ★7)
- **uv-publish**: `uv publish` 用于将构建的分发包发布到PyPI（Python Package Index）或其他索引服务器。 ([trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills](https://github.com/trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/uv-python-manager/references/commands/uv-publish.md) · ★7)
- **uv-python**: `uv python` 用于安装、管理和切换Python版本。它类似于pyenv，但速度更快，并且与uv的其他功能深度集成。 ([trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills](https://github.com/trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/uv-python-manager/references/commands/uv-python.md) · ★7)
- **uv-remove**: `uv remove` 用于从项目中移除依赖包，自动更新 `pyproject.toml` 和 `uv.lock` 文件。 ([trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills](https://github.com/trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/uv-python-manager/references/commands/uv-remove.md) · ★7)
- **uv-run**: `uv run` 用于运行Python脚本，自动处理依赖管理和虚拟环境。它可以运行带有内联依赖声明的脚本，无需手动管理虚拟环境。 ([trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills](https://github.com/trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/uv-python-manager/references/commands/uv-run.md) · ★7)
- **uv-setup**: Configure uv for Python project management. Use when creating a new Python project, migrating from pip/poetry/pipenv, managing dependencies, or setting up virtual environments. ([varrocen/claude-dev-setup](https://github.com/varrocen/claude-dev-setup/blob/HEAD/commands/uv-setup.md))
- **uv-sync**: `uv sync` 用于根据 `pyproject.toml` 和 `uv.lock` 同步虚拟环境，确保安装的包与锁文件一致。 ([trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills](https://github.com/trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/uv-python-manager/references/commands/uv-sync.md) · ★7)
- **uv-tool**: `uv tool` 用于安装和管理Python工具（类似pipx），可以在隔离环境中运行工具，无需激活虚拟环境。 ([trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills](https://github.com/trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/uv-python-manager/references/commands/uv-tool.md) · ★7)
- **uv-unwrap-checklist**: 检查 Blender 模型的 UV 展开质量，并给出可运行的 bpy 展开与打包脚本 ([bamboo-echo/pondera-plugins](https://github.com/bamboo-echo/pondera-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/uv-unwrap-checklist/commands/uv-unwrap-checklist.md))
- **uv-venv**: `uv venv` 用于创建和管理Python虚拟环境。它比传统的 `python -m venv` 更快，并且与现有虚拟环境工具完全兼容。 ([trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills](https://github.com/trotsky1997/My-Claude-Agent-Skills/blob/HEAD/uv-python-manager/references/commands/uv-venv.md) · ★7)
- **uvc**: Update documentation and push changes to version control (main repository) ([cruzanstx/daplug](https://github.com/cruzanstx/daplug/blob/HEAD/commands/uvc.md) · ★6)
